Surah: 56. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md (an earlier reader's proposal: ignore its judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

When your discovery is complete and before you write your final output, run this command once, with every Quran reference outside this surah that your output will use: `python3 /Volumes/aro/projects/prose_generation/_commentary/v16/missing.py S56 <refs separated by spaces>`. It lists refs from an earlier cross-reference list, by tier, that your refs do not include. That list is not authoritative and may be incomplete: judge each passage yourself, add it only where it supports or sharpens what you are writing, and leave the rest. To read a passage's Arabic before judging it, run `python3 /Volumes/aro/projects/prose_generation/_commentary/v16/missing.py text <refs>` (up to 40 refs per call) as often as you need. Run each command exactly as written, as the whole command: no cd, no && and no ; (such a command is refused and never runs). Also add any other passage you now recall that belongs, whether listed or not. Then write your final output. No other command or tool is available.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/map3/surah_map.md (adapted) =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), an earlier reader's channel review (channels.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not delegate, browse or inspect files; run only the command the header describes.

The channel review is an earlier reader's proposal. Ignore its judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what it already assembled: start from its chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where its wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the surah,
and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear. A member
need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah, not in one
word. A chain may join a sense and its reversal as well as the parts of one scene. Mark a member attested only
inside a fixed expression [fixed expression]; add no other label to a member or a chain, and where a source
records a phrase and rejects it, say so of that phrase alone. When the dictionary itself joins two of the
surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at
the level of its objects, their parts and their operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into
another's more abstract function unless nothing concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test,
however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain; for
   each, name the speaker and the situation in a few words, and include the ayah that opens its scene when the
   passage continues one.
2. `## Interactions`. Where chains meet: a shared member, a dictionary phrase that joins members of two chains,
   a Quran passage that stages two chains together, or one chain's scene needing another's. One line each: the
   chains, where they meet, the evidence.
3. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: the chains its words take part in, what its words add to each, and in one
   line the whole scene each chain makes across the surah, so that a writer who sees only this ayah sees the
   scene, not a fragment.
4. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel you did not carry into a chain: one short line
   each, its name and why, no prose.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.

===== _commentary/v16/work/s056/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 56

- 56:1 إِذَا وَقَعَتِ ٱلْوَاقِعَةُ
- 56:2 لَيْسَ لِوَقْعَتِهَا كَاذِبَةٌ
- 56:3 خَافِضَةٌۭ رَّافِعَةٌ
- 56:4 إِذَا رُجَّتِ ٱلْأَرْضُ رَجًّۭا
- 56:5 وَبُسَّتِ ٱلْجِبَالُ بَسًّۭا
- 56:6 فَكَانَتْ هَبَآءًۭ مُّنۢبَثًّۭا
- 56:7 وَكُنتُمْ أَزْوَٰجًۭا ثَلَٰثَةًۭ
- 56:8 فَأَصْحَٰبُ ٱلْمَيْمَنَةِ مَآ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلْمَيْمَنَةِ
- 56:9 وَأَصْحَٰبُ ٱلْمَشْـَٔمَةِ مَآ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلْمَشْـَٔمَةِ
- 56:10 وَٱلسَّٰبِقُونَ ٱلسَّٰبِقُونَ
- 56:11 أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ ٱلْمُقَرَّبُونَ
- 56:12 فِى جَنَّٰتِ ٱلنَّعِيمِ
- 56:13 ثُلَّةٌۭ مِّنَ ٱلْأَوَّلِينَ
- 56:14 وَقَلِيلٌۭ مِّنَ ٱلْءَاخِرِينَ
- 56:15 عَلَىٰ سُرُرٍۢ مَّوْضُونَةٍۢ
- 56:16 مُّتَّكِـِٔينَ عَلَيْهَا مُتَقَٰبِلِينَ
- 56:17 يَطُوفُ عَلَيْهِمْ وِلْدَٰنٌۭ مُّخَلَّدُونَ
- 56:18 بِأَكْوَابٍۢ وَأَبَارِيقَ وَكَأْسٍۢ مِّن مَّعِينٍۢ
- 56:19 لَّا يُصَدَّعُونَ عَنْهَا وَلَا يُنزِفُونَ
- 56:20 وَفَٰكِهَةٍۢ مِّمَّا يَتَخَيَّرُونَ
- 56:21 وَلَحْمِ طَيْرٍۢ مِّمَّا يَشْتَهُونَ
- 56:22 وَحُورٌ عِينٌۭ
- 56:23 كَأَمْثَٰلِ ٱللُّؤْلُؤِ ٱلْمَكْنُونِ
- 56:24 جَزَآءًۢ بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَعْمَلُونَ
- 56:25 لَا يَسْمَعُونَ فِيهَا لَغْوًۭا وَلَا تَأْثِيمًا
- 56:26 إِلَّا قِيلًۭا سَلَٰمًۭا سَلَٰمًۭا
- 56:27 وَأَصْحَٰبُ ٱلْيَمِينِ مَآ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلْيَمِينِ
- 56:28 فِى سِدْرٍۢ مَّخْضُودٍۢ
- 56:29 وَطَلْحٍۢ مَّنضُودٍۢ
- 56:30 وَظِلٍّۢ مَّمْدُودٍۢ
- 56:31 وَمَآءٍۢ مَّسْكُوبٍۢ
- 56:32 وَفَٰكِهَةٍۢ كَثِيرَةٍۢ
- 56:33 لَّا مَقْطُوعَةٍۢ وَلَا مَمْنُوعَةٍۢ
- 56:34 وَفُرُشٍۢ مَّرْفُوعَةٍ
- 56:35 إِنَّآ أَنشَأْنَٰهُنَّ إِنشَآءًۭ
- 56:36 فَجَعَلْنَٰهُنَّ أَبْكَارًا
- 56:37 عُرُبًا أَتْرَابًۭا
- 56:38 لِّأَصْحَٰبِ ٱلْيَمِينِ
- 56:39 ثُلَّةٌۭ مِّنَ ٱلْأَوَّلِينَ
- 56:40 وَثُلَّةٌۭ مِّنَ ٱلْءَاخِرِينَ
- 56:41 وَأَصْحَٰبُ ٱلشِّمَالِ مَآ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلشِّمَالِ
- 56:42 فِى سَمُومٍۢ وَحَمِيمٍۢ
- 56:43 وَظِلٍّۢ مِّن يَحْمُومٍۢ
- 56:44 لَّا بَارِدٍۢ وَلَا كَرِيمٍ
- 56:45 إِنَّهُمْ كَانُوا۟ قَبْلَ ذَٰلِكَ مُتْرَفِينَ
- 56:46 وَكَانُوا۟ يُصِرُّونَ عَلَى ٱلْحِنثِ ٱلْعَظِيمِ
- 56:47 وَكَانُوا۟ يَقُولُونَ أَئِذَا مِتْنَا وَكُنَّا تُرَابًۭا وَعِظَٰمًا أَءِنَّا لَمَبْعُوثُونَ
- 56:48 أَوَءَابَآؤُنَا ٱلْأَوَّلُونَ
- 56:49 قُلْ إِنَّ ٱلْأَوَّلِينَ وَٱلْءَاخِرِينَ
- 56:50 لَمَجْمُوعُونَ إِلَىٰ مِيقَٰتِ يَوْمٍۢ مَّعْلُومٍۢ
- 56:51 ثُمَّ إِنَّكُمْ أَيُّهَا ٱلضَّآلُّونَ ٱلْمُكَذِّبُونَ
- 56:52 لَءَاكِلُونَ مِن شَجَرٍۢ مِّن زَقُّومٍۢ
- 56:53 فَمَالِـُٔونَ مِنْهَا ٱلْبُطُونَ
- 56:54 فَشَٰرِبُونَ عَلَيْهِ مِنَ ٱلْحَمِيمِ
- 56:55 فَشَٰرِبُونَ شُرْبَ ٱلْهِيمِ
- 56:56 هَٰذَا نُزُلُهُمْ يَوْمَ ٱلدِّينِ
- 56:57 نَحْنُ خَلَقْنَٰكُمْ فَلَوْلَا تُصَدِّقُونَ
- 56:58 أَفَرَءَيْتُم مَّا تُمْنُونَ
- 56:59 ءَأَنتُمْ تَخْلُقُونَهُۥٓ أَمْ نَحْنُ ٱلْخَٰلِقُونَ
- 56:60 نَحْنُ قَدَّرْنَا بَيْنَكُمُ ٱلْمَوْتَ وَمَا نَحْنُ بِمَسْبُوقِينَ
- 56:61 عَلَىٰٓ أَن نُّبَدِّلَ أَمْثَٰلَكُمْ وَنُنشِئَكُمْ فِى مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ
- 56:62 وَلَقَدْ عَلِمْتُمُ ٱلنَّشْأَةَ ٱلْأُولَىٰ فَلَوْلَا تَذَكَّرُونَ
- 56:63 أَفَرَءَيْتُم مَّا تَحْرُثُونَ
- 56:64 ءَأَنتُمْ تَزْرَعُونَهُۥٓ أَمْ نَحْنُ ٱلزَّٰرِعُونَ
- 56:65 لَوْ نَشَآءُ لَجَعَلْنَٰهُ حُطَٰمًۭا فَظَلْتُمْ تَفَكَّهُونَ
- 56:66 إِنَّا لَمُغْرَمُونَ
- 56:67 بَلْ نَحْنُ مَحْرُومُونَ
- 56:68 أَفَرَءَيْتُمُ ٱلْمَآءَ ٱلَّذِى تَشْرَبُونَ
- 56:69 ءَأَنتُمْ أَنزَلْتُمُوهُ مِنَ ٱلْمُزْنِ أَمْ نَحْنُ ٱلْمُنزِلُونَ
- 56:70 لَوْ نَشَآءُ جَعَلْنَٰهُ أُجَاجًۭا فَلَوْلَا تَشْكُرُونَ
- 56:71 أَفَرَءَيْتُمُ ٱلنَّارَ ٱلَّتِى تُورُونَ
- 56:72 ءَأَنتُمْ أَنشَأْتُمْ شَجَرَتَهَآ أَمْ نَحْنُ ٱلْمُنشِـُٔونَ
- 56:73 نَحْنُ جَعَلْنَٰهَا تَذْكِرَةًۭ وَمَتَٰعًۭا لِّلْمُقْوِينَ
- 56:74 فَسَبِّحْ بِٱسْمِ رَبِّكَ ٱلْعَظِيمِ
- 56:75 ۞ فَلَآ أُقْسِمُ بِمَوَٰقِعِ ٱلنُّجُومِ
- 56:76 وَإِنَّهُۥ لَقَسَمٌۭ لَّوْ تَعْلَمُونَ عَظِيمٌ
- 56:77 إِنَّهُۥ لَقُرْءَانٌۭ كَرِيمٌۭ
- 56:78 فِى كِتَٰبٍۢ مَّكْنُونٍۢ
- 56:79 لَّا يَمَسُّهُۥٓ إِلَّا ٱلْمُطَهَّرُونَ
- 56:80 تَنزِيلٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّ ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ
- 56:81 أَفَبِهَٰذَا ٱلْحَدِيثِ أَنتُم مُّدْهِنُونَ
- 56:82 وَتَجْعَلُونَ رِزْقَكُمْ أَنَّكُمْ تُكَذِّبُونَ
- 56:83 فَلَوْلَآ إِذَا بَلَغَتِ ٱلْحُلْقُومَ
- 56:84 وَأَنتُمْ حِينَئِذٍۢ تَنظُرُونَ
- 56:85 وَنَحْنُ أَقْرَبُ إِلَيْهِ مِنكُمْ وَلَٰكِن لَّا تُبْصِرُونَ
- 56:86 فَلَوْلَآ إِن كُنتُمْ غَيْرَ مَدِينِينَ
- 56:87 تَرْجِعُونَهَآ إِن كُنتُمْ صَٰدِقِينَ
- 56:88 فَأَمَّآ إِن كَانَ مِنَ ٱلْمُقَرَّبِينَ
- 56:89 فَرَوْحٌۭ وَرَيْحَانٌۭ وَجَنَّتُ نَعِيمٍۢ
- 56:90 وَأَمَّآ إِن كَانَ مِنْ أَصْحَٰبِ ٱلْيَمِينِ
- 56:91 فَسَلَٰمٌۭ لَّكَ مِنْ أَصْحَٰبِ ٱلْيَمِينِ
- 56:92 وَأَمَّآ إِن كَانَ مِنَ ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ ٱلضَّآلِّينَ
- 56:93 فَنُزُلٌۭ مِّنْ حَمِيمٍۢ
- 56:94 وَتَصْلِيَةُ جَحِيمٍ
- 56:95 إِنَّ هَٰذَا لَهُوَ حَقُّ ٱلْيَقِينِ
- 56:96 فَسَبِّحْ بِٱسْمِ رَبِّكَ ٱلْعَظِيمِ


===== _commentary/v16/work/s056/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

## و ق ع (root_001675): 56:1 وَقَعَتِ, 56:1 ٱلْوَاقِعَةُ, 56:2 لِوَقْعَتِهَا, 56:75 بِمَوَٰقِعِ

- **B001** düşüp gerçekleşme — düşmek ve yerini bulmak · sözün veya hükmün kesinleşmesi · başlarına gelmek · hemen secdeye kapanmak
  وقع الشيء وقوعا فهو واقع (maqayis)؛ وقع الشيء موقعه (sihah)؛ وقع القول والحكم إذا وجب (tahdhib)؛ الوقوع ثبوت الشيء وسقوطه (mufradat)
- **B002** ağır felaket — büyük hesap günü veya ağır felaket
  الواقعة القيامة لأنها تقع بالخلق فتغشاهم (maqayis)؛ الواقعة القيامة (sihah)؛ الواقعة النازلة من صروف الدهر (tahdhib)؛ الواقعة لا تقال إلا في الشدة والمكروه (mufradat)
- **B003** yağışın düşmesi ve düştüğü yer — yağmurun damla damla düşmesi · sonbaharın ilk yağmurunun toprağa düşmesi · yağışın düştüğü yerler
  وقع الغيث سقط متفرقا ومواقع الغيث مساقطه (maqayis)؛ وقع المطر (ayn)؛ مواقع الغيث مساقطه (sihah)؛ وقع ربيع بالأرض لأول مطر يقع في الخريف (tahdhib)؛ وقع المطر نحو سقط ومواقع الغيث مساقطه (mufradat)
- **B004** çarpma ve çarpma sesi — vuruş veya çarpma sesi
  الوقع وقعة الضرب بالشيء (ayn)؛ سمعت وقع المطر وهو شدة ضربه الأرض (tahdhib)؛ سمعت لحوافر الدواب وقعا ووقوعا (tahdhib)؛ وقع الحديد صوته (mufradat)
- **B005** savaşta çarpışma ve düşmana saldırma — savaş çarpışması · savaşta karşılaşma · bir topluluğa savaşta saldırıp zarar vermek · savaşta karşılıklı çarpışmak
  الوقعة صدمة الحرب (maqayis)؛ الوقعة صدمة الحرب والوقيعة القتال (sihah)؛ وقع بهم وأوقع بهم في الحرب (tahdhib)؛ المواقعة في الحرب والإيقاع في شن الحرب بالوقعة (mufradat)
- **B006** kuşun konması ve konduğu yer — kuşun yere, ağaca veya yuvasına konması · kanatlarını kapatmış kuşa benzetilen yıldız · kuşun alıştığı konma yeri
  من وقع الطائر (maqayis)؛ موقعة الطائر موضعه الذي يقع عليه (maqayis)؛ يقال للطير إذا كان على أرض أو شجر هن وقوع (ayn)؛ ميقعة البازي الموضع الذي يألفه فيقع عليه (sihah)؛ طائر واقع إذا كان على شجر أو موكن (tahdhib)؛ الموضع الذي يستقر فيه الطير موقع (mufradat)
- **B007** hayvanın yere çöküp yatması [kalıp] — devenin çökmesi, hayvanın yere yatması
  وقعت الدواب والإبل أي ربضت تشبيها بوقوع الطير (ayn)؛ يقال للإبل إذا بركت والدواب إذا ربضت قد وقعت (tahdhib)
- **B008** su birikintisi yeri ve su tutan toprak — dağınık su birikme yerleri · kayada su biriken oyuk · suyu emmeyip tutan toprak veya çayır

  الآخر نقيض المتقدم؛ الآخر تال للأول؛ أخر جماعة أخرى (maqayis); هذا آخر وهذه أخرى؛ الآخر والآخرة نقيض المتقدم والمتقدمة؛ الآخر الغائب؛ أخر جماعة أخرى (ayn); الآخر بعد الأول؛ الآخر أحد الشيئين؛ الجمع أواخر؛ أخريات الناس أي أواخرهم؛ أخرى القوم أي من كان في آخرهم؛ أبعد الله الاخر (sihah); معنى آخر شيء غير الأول الذي قبله؛ أخر جماعة أخرى؛ أخرى القوم أي في أواخرهم (tahdhib); آخر يقابل به الأول، وآخر يقابل به الواحد؛ أخر معدول (mufradat)
- **B002** geciktirme veya gecikme — geciktirme · geciktirmek; sonraya bırakmak · gecikmek; geride kalmak · geç vakitte; sonradan · vadeli satmak · ürünü hasadın sonuna kadar kalan hurma ağacı
  تأخر أخرا؛ بعتك بيعا بأخرة أي نظرة؛ ما عرفته إلا بأخرة (maqayis); بعته الشيء بأخرة أي بتأخير؛ تأخر أخرا؛ جاء فلان أخيرا أي بأخرة (ayn); أخرته فتأخر؛ واستأخر مثل تأخر؛ بعته بأخرة وبنظرة أي بنسيئة؛ المئخار النخلة التي يبقى حملها إلى آخر الصرام (sihah); المستأخر نقيض المستقدم؛ بعته سلعة بأخرة أي بتأخير؛ بأخرة وبنظرة؛ المئخار النخلة التي يبقى حملها إلى آخر الصرام (tahdhib); التأخير مقابل للتقديم؛ إنما يؤخرهم؛ أخرنا إلى أجل قريب؛ بعته بأخرة أي بتأخير أجل (mufradat)
- **B003** arka bölüm — nesnenin arka bölümü · gözün şakağa yakın arka köşesi · binek semerinin arka dayanağı · semerin arka dayanağı için seyrek ve tartışmalı söyleyiş · arka tarafından; arkasından · dişi devenin iki arka yanı
  آخرة الرحل وقادمته ومؤخر الرحل ومقدمه؛ مؤخر العين ومقدم العين (maqayis); مقدم الشيء ومؤخره؛ آخرة الرجل وقادمته؛ مقدم العين ومؤخرها؛ مؤخر الشيء ومقدمه (ayn); شق ثوبه أخرا ومن أخر أي من مؤخره؛ مؤخر العين؛ مؤخرة الرحل؛ مؤخر الشئ بالتشديد نقيض مقدمه (sihah); آخرة الرحل وقادمته ومؤخر العين ومقدمها؛ مؤخر الشيء ومقدمه؛ نظر إلي بمؤخر عينه؛ شق ثوبه أخرا ومن أخر؛ للناقة آخران وقادمان؛ مؤخرة الرحل وآخرة الرحل (tahdhib)
- **B004** ölümden sonraki yaşam ve öteki dünya — ölümden sonraki yaşam; öteki dünya · öteki dünya
  يعبر بالدار الآخرة عن النشأة الثانية؛ الدار الآخرة؛ الآخرة؛ تقدير الإضافة دار الحياة الآخرة (mufradat)

## س ر ر (root_000697): 56:15 سُرُرٍ

- **B001** saklama ve gizli paylaşım — gizlenen bilgi veya durum · kişinin gizli iç durumu veya gizlice yaptığı iş · bir şeyi gizleyip saklamak · birine bir sözü gizlice açmak · kulağına gizlice söylemek · kendi aralarında gizlice konuşmak · gizlice konuşmaya yarayan tomar benzeri araç
  السر خلاف الإعلان (maqayis)؛ السر ما أسررت والسريرة عمل السر (ayn)؛ السر الذي يكتم والسريرة مثله (sihah)؛ الإسرار خلاف الإعلان والسر هو الحديث المكتم في النفس (mufradat)؛ ساره في أذنه وتساروا (sihah)
- **B002** açığa vurma, tartışmalı kullanım — 
  أسررته أعلنته (maqayis)؛ أسررت الشيء أظهرته وكتمته أيضا (jamhara)؛ أسررت الشيء كتمته وأعلنته أيضا (sihah)؛ قال الفراء أخطأ أبو عبيدة (maqayis)؛ لم أسمع ذلك لغيره (tahdhib)
- **B003** gizli tutulan evlilik veya cinsel ilişki — 
  السر وهو النكاح (maqayis)؛ السر الجماع والسر الذكر (sihah)؛ السر النكاح والزنى وخطبة المعتدة (tahdhib)؛ كني عن النكاح بالسر من حيث إنه يخفى (mufradat)
- **B004** ayın görünmediği ay sonu — ayın sonunda hilalin görünmediği bir veya iki günlük dönem · ayın son gecesi
  السرار ليلة يستسر الهلال (maqayis)؛ السرار يوم يستسر فيه الهلال آخر يوم من الشهر (ayn)؛ سرر الشهر آخر ليلة منه وكذلك سراره (sihah)؛ السرار اليوم الذي يستتر فيه القمر آخر الشهر (mufradat)
- **B005** bir şeyin arı özü veya en seçkin bölümü [kalıp] — bir şeyin katkısız özü · topluluğunun merkezindeki en seçkin kesim · soyun katkısız ve en seçkin kolu · vadinin toprağı en iyi veya en elverişli yeri · bir şeyin özü ve üstün niteliğinin çekirdeği
  السر خالص الشيء وسر النسب (maqayis)؛ سر كل شيء خالصه وسر الوادي وسراره أطيبه ترابا (jamhara)؛ في سر قومه أي في أوسطهم وسر الوادي أفضل موضع (sihah)؛ استعير للخالص ومنه سر الوادي وسرارته (mufradat)
- **B006** göbek ve kesilen göbek bağı parçası — göbek · bebekten kesilen göbek bağı parçası · bebeğin göbek bağı parçasını kesmek
  السرة سرة الإنسان (maqayis)؛ السرة في البطن موضع السرر الذي يقطع من الصبي (jamhara)؛ السر ما تقطعه القابلة من سرة الصبي (sihah)؛ سرة البطن ما يبقى بعد القطع والسر والسرر لما يقطع منها (mufradat)
- **B007** devede gövde içi ağrı hastalığı — devede göbek, göğüs veya göğüs altı ağrısı · bu gövde ağrısına tutulmuş deve
  السرر داء يأخذ البعير في سرته (maqayis)؛ السرر داء يصيب الإبل في صدورها (jamhara)؛ بعير أسر وناقة سراء (sihah)؛ وجع يأخذ في الكركرة (tahdhib)
- **B008** içi oyuk olma ve oyuğa çubuk yerleştirme — ateş çubuğunun oyuğuna tutuşturma çubuğu yerleştirmek · içi oyuk boru biçimli çubuk · içi oyuk kişi
  سررت الزند وذلك أن يبقى أسر أي أجوف (maqayis)؛ قناة سراء أي جوفاء (maqayis)؛ سر زندك فإنه أسر أي أجوف (sihah)؛ رجل أسر إذا كان أجوف (tahdhib)
- **B009** avuç ve alın çizgileri — avuç içi çizgileri · alın veya yüz çizgileri ve kırışıklıkları
  الأسرار خطوط باطن الراحة (maqayis)؛ السر والسرار والجميع الأسرار خطوط راحة الكف (ayn)؛ السرر واحد أسرار الكف والجبهة (sihah)؛ أسرة الراحة وأسارير الجبهة (mufradat)
- **B010** sevinç ve gönence — üzüntüden uzak iç sevinci · beni sevindirdi · rahatlık, bolluk ve gönence · iyilik eden ve sevindiren kişi
  السرور أمر خال من الحزن (maqayis)؛ السر ضد الضر وقال قوم السر والسرور واحد (jamhara)؛ السراء الرخاء نقيض الضراء (sihah)؛ السرور ما ينكتم من الفرح (mufradat)
- **B011** oturma, yaslanma veya dinlenme yeri — oturulan, yaslanılan veya yatılan yer · başın dayandığı yer · yaşamın yerleşik rahatlığı ve dinginliği
  السرير وجمعه سرر وأسرة (maqayis)؛ سرير الرأس مستقره (maqayis)؛ السرير معروف والعدد أسرة والجميع السرر (tahdhib)؛ السرير الذي يجلس عليه من السرور (mufradat)
- **B012** bitkinin nemli üst bölümleri [kalıp] — bitkilerin nemli uçları veya gövdelerinin üst yarıları
  أطراف الريحان تسمى سرورا لأنها أرطب شيء فيه (maqayis)؛ السرور من النبات أنصاف سوقها العلى (tahdhib)
- **B013** yer mantarı üzerindeki kabuk ve toprak [kalıp] — yer mantarı üzerindeki kabuk, çamur ve toprak
  السرر ما على الكمأة من القشور والطين (sihah)؛ السرار ما على الكمأة من القشور والتراب (tahdhib)
- **B014** işlerin inceliğini bilen becerikli kişi — işlerin inceliğini bilen kavrayışlı kişi · sevdiğim ve çok yakın bulduğum kişi
  السرسور العالم الفطن (maqayis)؛ السرسور العالم الفطن الدخال في الأمور (sihah)؛ سرسور هذا الأمر إذا كان عالما به (tahdhib)؛ سرسوري وسرسورتي أي حبيبي وخاصتي (tahdhib)
- **B015** tepecik üzerindeki kum tabakası — küçük bir tepenin üzerindeki kum
  السري ما على الأكمة من الرمل (maqayis)

## و ض ن (root_001658): 56:15 مَّوْضُونَةٍ

- **B001** sıkı ve iç içe dokuma — sıkı ve iç içe dokuma · değerli taşlarla iç içe dokunmuş yataklar · sıkı ve iç içe dokunmuş · sıkı dokunmuş olan
  الوضن نسج السرير وشبهه بالجوهر والثياب فهو موضون (ayn); على سرر موضونة أي منسوجة بالدرر بعضها في بعض مضاعف (ayn); ويقال أيضا منسوجة بالجواهر ومنه قوله تعالى على سرر موضونة (sihah); الموضونة المنسوجة (tahdhib); يستعار لكل نسج محكم (mufradat)
- **B002** deve takımı için örülmüş geniş kayış kuşağı — deve takımı için iç içe örülmüş geniş kayış kuşağı · örülü kayış kuşakları · örülü kayış kuşakları · kayış bağını ördü
  الوضين بطان البعير إذا كان منسوجا بعضه في بعض يكون من السيور (ayn); الوضين للهودج بمنزلة البطان للقتب والتصدير للرحل والحزام للسرج (sihah); من السيور إذا نسج نساجة بعضه على بعض مضاعفا (sihah); وضين الناقة وضينا لأنه منسوج (tahdhib); الوضين وهو حزام الرحل وجمعه وضن (mufradat)
- **B003** halkaları iç içe örülmüş sık dokulu zırh — halkaları iç içe örülmüş zırh · halkaları sık aralıklı örülmüş zırh · zırh halkalarını iç içe örme
  الموضونة أيضا الدرع المنسوجة توضن حلق الدرع بعضها في بعض مضاعفة (sihah); الموضونة الدرع المنسوجة (tahdhib); درع موضونة مقاربة النسج (tahdhib); الوضن نسج الدرع (mufradat)
- **B004** parçaları yaklaştırıp üst üste dizme — taş ve tuğlayı birbirine geçirerek üst üste dizdi · birbirine geçirilip üst üste dizilmiş · üst üste dizme ve birbirine yaklaştırma · ev eşyasını birbirine yaklaştır · eşyasını üst üste dizdi
  وضن فلان الحجر والآجر بعضه فوق بعض إذا أشرجه فهو موضون (tahdhib); ضنيه يعني متاع بيتها أي قاربي بعضه من بعض (tahdhib); الوضن النضد يقال وضن متاعه بعضه فوق بعض (tahdhib)
- **B005** biçime bağlı adlandırmalar — sepet · örme sandalye
  الميضانة القفة وهي المرجونة والقفعة (tahdhib); الوضنة الكرسي المنسوج (tahdhib)
- **B006** sevgi göstererek yakınlaşma ya da kendini alçaltma — sevgi gösterip yakınlaşma ve kendini alçaltma
  التوضن التحبب والتوضن التذلل (tahdhib)

## و ك ء (root_001678): 56:16 مُّتَّكِـِٔينَ

- **B001** kap agzini sikica baglayan bag — tulum ya da kap agzini sikica baglayan ip veya kayis · tulumu ya da kabi agiz bagiyla sikica baglamak
  الوكاء الذي يشد به (maqayis)؛ الوكاء كل سير أو خيط يشد به السقاء أو الوعاء (tahdhib)؛ الوكاء رباط الشيء (mufradat)
- **B002** vermekten kacinma veya agzini tutup susma — cimrilik edip vermekten kacinmak · elinden hicbir sey cikarmayan cimri · agzini tutup konusmamak · agzini kapa ve sus · iki yer arasinda konusmamak
  سألته فأوكى على أي بخل (maqayis)؛ فلان لوكاء ما يبض بشيء (maqayis)؛ يوكي فاه فلا يتكلم (tahdhib)؛ أوك حلقك أي شد فمك واسكت (tahdhib)
- **B003** arayi siddetli yuruyusle butunuyle katetme — iki yer arasini siddetli yuruyusle butunuyle katetmek · yuruyusunu sertlestirip kendini zorlayan kisi
  يوكى بين الصفا والمروة أي يملأ ما بينهما سعيا (maqayis)؛ الإيكاء في كلام العرب يكون بمعنى السعي الشديد (tahdhib)؛ الموكي الذي يتشدد في مشيه (tahdhib)؛ يملأ ما بينهما سعيا كما يوكى السقاء بعد الملء (mufradat)
- **B004** yaslanma, dayanak saglama ve yaslandirma — bir seye yaslanip ondan destek almak · bir seye dayanip yaslanmak · cok yaslanan kimse · yaslanilan sey · yaslanilan yer ya da dayanak · oturulan yer · birine yaslanacagi dayanak hazirlamak · birini destek uzerine koyup yaslandirmak · birini yaslanan kisi durusuna sokmak
  توكأت على كذا أي اتكأت (maqayis)؛ التوكؤ التحامل على العصا (ayn;tahdhib)؛ اتكأ على الشيء فهو متكئ والموضع متكأ (sihah)؛ أوكأت فلانا إيكاء إذا نصبت له متكأ (ayn;sihah;tahdhib)؛ توكأ على العصا اعتمد بها وتشدد بها (mufradat)
- **B005** cinsel birlesmeye siddetle ihtiyac duyma [kalıp] — cinsel birlesmeye siddetle ihtiyac duyan
  فلان موكي الغلمة ومزك الغلمة ومشط الغلمة إذا كانت به حاجة شديدة إلى الخلاط (tahdhib)
- **B006** dogum sancisinda devenin kivranmasi [kalıp] — devenin dogum sancisinda kivranip carpinmasi
  توكأت الناقة وهو تصلقها عند مخاضها (ayn;tahdhib)
- **B007** dolup dolgunlasma veya bosaltimin tutulmasi — develerin yaglanip dolgunlasmasi · insanin karninin tutulup diski cikaramamasi · tulumun ya da benzeri bir kabin dolmasi · kalin derili tulum agzi icin kullanilan, anlami belirsiz niteleme
  استوكت الإبل استيكاء إذا امتلأت سمنا (tahdhib)؛ استوكى بطن الإنسان وهو أن لا يخرج منه نجوه (tahdhib)؛ للسقاء ونحوه إذا امتلأ قد استوكى (tahdhib)

## ق ب ل (root_001198): 56:16 مُتَقَٰبِلِينَ, 56:45 قَبْلَ

- **B001** karşı karşıya olma ve ön yön — ön taraf, karşıya dönük yön · ön taraftaki cinsel bölge · yüz yüze, göz göre göre yapmak · karşısında, tam karşı hizada · dağın karşıdan görünen yamacı veya yükseltisi · yönelecek bir yönü yok; işin yolunu bulamıyor
  مواجهة الشيء للشيء (maqayis)؛ القبل خلاف الدبر (ayn;tahdhib)؛ قبل ضد الدبر (jamhara)؛ المقابلة المواجهة (sihah)؛ الإقبال التوجه نحو القبل (mufradat)
- **B002** önce olma ve sırada yaklaşma — önce; zaman, yer veya sırada önde · gelecek veya yaklaşan yıl, gece ve benzeri dönem · bundan sonraki zamanda · gençliğinin başında, yaşlılık izi belirmemiş
  قبل الذي هو خلاف بعد (maqayis)؛ من قبل ومن بعد غايتان (ayn;tahdhib)؛ قبل ضد بعد (jamhara)؛ القابلة الليلة المقبلة والعام القابل المقبل (sihah;tahdhib)؛ قبل يستعمل في التقدم المتصل والمنفصل (mufradat)
- **B003** birinin tarafından veya nezdinde [kalıp] — o kişiden, onun tarafından veya yanından · o kişide hakkım var; ondan alacağım var
  هذا من قبل فلان أي من عنده (maqayis)؛ أصيب هذا من قبله أي من تلقائه ومن لدنه (ayn;tahdhib)؛ لي قبل فلان حق أي عنده (sihah;mufradat)
- **B004** uygun bulup benimseme — bir şeyi uygun bulup benimsemek · olumlu karşılayıp benimsemek ve karşılığını vermek · göze ve gönle hoş gelmek
  قبلت الشيء قبولا (maqayis)؛ التقبل القبول (ayn)؛ تقبلت الشيء وقبلته قبولا (sihah)؛ قبلت الشيء قبولا إذا رضيته (tahdhib)؛ قبلت عذره وتوبته وغيره وتقبلته (mufradat)
- **B005** namazda yönelinen yön — namazda yönelinen yön veya yer
  القبلة سميت قبلة لإقبال الناس عليها في صلاتهم (maqayis)؛ القبلة قبلة الصلاة (jamhara)؛ القبلة التي يصلى نحوها (sihah)؛ صار اسما للمكان المقابل المتوجه إليه للصلاة (mufradat)
- **B006** öpücük ve öpme — öpücük; dudakla öpmek
  الفعل من القبلة التقبيل (ayn)؛ القبلة من التقبيل معروفة (sihah)؛ القبلة معروفة وجمعها القبل وفعلها التقبيل (tahdhib)؛ ومنه القبلة وجمعها قبل وقبلته تقبيلا (mufradat)
- **B007** çıkanı karşılayıp teslim alma — doğumda bebeği karşılayıp alan kadın · kuyudan çıkan kovayı teslim alan kişi
  القابلة التي تقبل الولد عند الولاد (maqayis)؛ القابلة التي تقبل الولد عند الولاد (ayn)؛ القابلة التي تقبل الصبي (jamhara)؛ القابلة من النساء معروفة (sihah)؛ القابل الذي يستقبل الدلو من البئر (mufradat)
- **B008** güvence ve sorumluluk üstlenme — başkası için güvence veren kişi · güvence üstlenme veya yazılı yüklenim
  القبيل الكفيل يقال قبل به قبالة (maqayis)؛ القبيل الكفيل (jamhara)؛ القبيل الكفيل والعريف (sihah)؛ قبل به يقبل به قبالة إذا كفل به (tahdhib)؛ قيل للكفالة قبالة (mufradat)

## ك ث ر (root_001286): 56:32 كَثِيرَةٍ

- **B001** çokluk ve sayıca artma — çokluk; sayının artması ve azlığın karşıtı · bir şey çoğaldı, sayısı arttı · çok, sayıca fazla · bir şeyi çoğaltmak · bir şeyden çokça edinmek veya onu çok saymak · malın ya da durumun azı ve çoğu · pek çok, çok büyük sayıda
  الكثرة نماء العدد (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الكثير ضد القليل (jamhara)؛ الكثرة نقيض القلة (sihah)؛ أصل صحيح يدل خلاف القلة (maqayis)؛ الكثرة والقلة يستعملان في الكمية المنفصلة كالأعداد (mufradat)؛ كثر الشيء كثرة فهو كثير (ayn;sihah;tahdhib)؛ أكثرت الشيء وكثرته جعلته كثيرا (ayn;tahdhib)؛ استكثرت من الشيء أي أكثرت منه (sihah)؛ عدد كثار وكثير وكاثر (jamhara;sihah;mufradat;maqayis)

  كاثرناهم فكثرناهم (ayn;sihah;tahdhib)؛ كاثر بنو فلان بني فلان فكثروهم إذا زادوا على عددهم (jamhara)؛ كاثر بنو فلان بني فلان فكثروهم أي كانوا أكثر منهم (maqayis)؛ كاثرناهم فكثرناهم أي غلبناهم بالكثرة (sihah)؛ التكاثر المكاثرة (sihah)؛ التفاخر بكثرة العدد والمال (tahdhib)؛ المكاثرة والتكاثر التباري في كثرة المال والعز (mufradat)؛ فلان مكثور أي مغلوب في الكثرة (mufradat)
- **B003** kişiye bağlı çokluk nitelemeleri [kalıp] — malı çok kişi · çok konuşan kadın veya erkek · iyilik isteyenleri veya üzerindeki haklar çoğalmış kişi · başkasının malıyla kendini varlıklı göstermek
  رجل مكثر كثير المال (ayn;tahdhib)؛ أكثر الرجل أي كثر ماله (sihah)؛ رجل كاثر إذا كان كثير المال (mufradat)؛ رجل مكثار وامرأة مكثار وهما الكثيرا الكلام (ayn)؛ رجل مكثار وامرأة مكثار إذا كانا كثيري الكلام (tahdhib)؛ المكثار متعارف في كثرة الكلام (mufradat)؛ رجل مكثور عليه أي كثر من يطلب إليه معروفه (ayn;tahdhib)؛ مكثور عليه إذا نفد ما عنده وكثرت عليه الحقوق (sihah)؛ فلان يتكثر بمال غيره (sihah)
- **B004** özel ırmak veya bol iyilik — cennetteki özel ırmak · bol veya büyük iyilik · iyiliği ve bağışı bol, cömert önder
  الكوثر نهر في الجنة يتشعب منه أكثر أنهار الجنة (ayn)؛ الكوثر الخير الكثير الذي أعطاه النبي (ayn)؛ الكوثر من الرجال السيد الكثير الخير (sihah)؛ الكوثر نهر في الجنة وأراد الخير الكثير (maqayis)؛ الكوثر هو الخير الكثير (tahdhib)؛ الكوثر فوعل من الكثرة ومعناه الخير الكثير (tahdhib)؛ الكوثر الرجل الكثير العطاء والخير والسيد (tahdhib)؛ قيل هو نهر في الجنة وقيل الخير العظيم (mufradat)؛ يقال للرجل السخي كوثر (mufradat)
- **B005** kabarıp yükselen yoğun toz — kabarıp yükselen yoğun toz · son derece çoğalmak
  الكوثر من الغبار الكثير وقد تكوثر (sihah)؛ يقال للغبار إذا سطع وكثر كوثر (tahdhib)؛ الكوثر الغبار سمي بذلك لكثرته وثورانه (maqayis)؛ تكوثر الشيء كثر كثرة متناهية (mufradat)؛ ثار نقع الموت حتى تكوثرا (sihah;mufradat)
- **B006** hurma ağacının iç göbeği — hurma ağacının iç göbeği; bazı açıklamalarda ilk çiçek sürgünü · meyve veya hurma göbeği için el kesme cezası yoktur · hurma ağacı çiçek sürgünü verdi
  الكثر والكثر جمار النخل ويقال الكثر الجذب وهو الجمار أيضا (ayn)؛ الكثر الجمار وقال قوم هو الكثر بفتح الثاء (jamhara)؛ لا قطع في ثمر ولا كثر (jamhara;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ الكثر جمار النخل ويقال طلعها (sihah)؛ الكثر جمار النخل في كلام الأنصار وهو الجذب أيضا (tahdhib)؛ الكثر الجمار الكثير وحكي بتسكين الثاء (mufradat)

## ق ط ع (root_001240): 56:33 مَقْطُوعَةٍ

- **B001** kesip ayırmak — bir şeyi kesip ayırmak · eli kesilmiş kimse

  منقطع كل شيء حيث تنتهي غايته؛ انقطع الشيء ذهب وقته؛ انقطع البرد والحر (ayn)؛ قطع ماء الركية أي انقطع وذهب؛ منقطع الرمل حيث ينقطع ولا رمل خلفه؛ منقطع كل شيء حيث ينتهى إليه طرفه (sihah)؛ منقطع كل شيء حيث ينقطع مثل منقطع الرمل والحرة؛ أقطعت السماء إذا انقطع المطر؛ قطع ماء قليبكم إذا قل ماؤها وذهب (tahdhib)؛ أصاب بئرهم قطع أي انقطع ماؤها؛ مقاطع الأودية مآخيرها (mufradat)؛ منقطع الرمل ومقطعه حيث ينقطع؛ أصاب بئر فلان قطع إذا نقص ماؤها (maqayis)

  قاطع الرحم؛ قطع رحمه إذا هجرها؛ الأقطوعة علامة أنها صارمتها؛ الهجر مقطعة للود (ayn)؛ قطع رحمه قطيعة؛ رحم قطعاء؛ الأقطوعة علامة للصريمة والهجران؛ التقاطع ضد التواصل (sihah)؛ تقطعت أسبابهم ووصلهم؛ قطعت حبال مودتها؛ قطع فلان رحمه؛ اتقوا القطيعاء أي أن ينقطع بعضكم من بعض في الحرب (tahdhib)؛ قطع الوصل هو الهجران؛ قطع الرحم يكون بالهجران ومنع البر (mufradat)؛ القطيعة الهجران؛ تقاطع الرجلان إذا تصارما؛ أقطوعة علامة للصريمة (maqayis)
- **B008** bölüp dağıtmak [kalıp] — işlerini kendi aralarında bölüp ayırmak · onları yeryüzünde gruplara ayırmak · topluluğun dağılıp ayrılması

  قطع الرجل بحبل أي اختنق؛ ثم ليقطع أي ليختنق (ayn)؛ ثم ليقطع قالوا ليختنق؛ يقطع نفسه من الأرض حتى يختنق (sihah)؛ أجمع المفسرون على أن تأويل ثم ليقطع ثم ليختنق؛ يقطع حياته ونفسه خنقا (tahdhib)؛ ليقطع أجله بالاختناق؛ ثم ليختنق (mufradat)؛ في قوله ثم ليقطع إنه الاختناق (maqayis)

- **B011** kökünü kazımak [kalıp] — bir soyun devamını yok etmek · bir topluluğun bir bölümünü yok etmek
  ليقطع طرفا أي يهلك جماعة منهم؛ قطع دابر الإنسان هو إفناء نوعه؛ إلا أن تقطع قلوبهم أي إلا أن يموتوا وقيل إلا أن يتوبوا توبة بها تنقطع قلوبهم ندما
- **B012** giysi biçip dikmek [kalıp] — onlara giysi biçip dikildi · biçilmiş veya kısa giysiler · kumaşın gömlek olmaya yetmesi
  المقطعات من الثياب شبه الجباب؛ الثياب المختلفة الألوان؛ القطع من الثياب ضرب منها (ayn)؛ المقطعات من الثياب شبه الجباب؛ مقطعات الثياب والشعر قصارها؛ هذا الثوب يقطعك قميصا (sihah)؛ قطعت لهم ثياب من نار أي خيطت وسويت وجعلت لبوسا؛ المقطعات من الثياب كل ثوب يقطع من قميص وغيره؛ الثوب يقطعك قميصا (tahdhib)؛ قطع الثوب؛ قطعت لهم ثياب من نار (mufradat)؛ هذا الثوب يقطعك قميصا؛ المقطعات الثياب القصار (maqayis)

  أقطع الوالي قطيعة أي طائفة من أرض الخراج؛ أقطعني قضبانا أذن لي قطعها؛ أقطع فلان من مال فلان طائفة (ayn)؛ أقطعته قضبانا أي أذنت له في قطعها؛ أقطعته قطيعة أي طائفة من أرض الخراج؛ اقتطعت قطيعا من غنم فلان (sihah)؛ أقطعني فلان نهرا إذا أذن له في حفره؛ أقطعني قضبانا من كرمه؛ قاطعت فلانا على كذا من الأجر والعمل (tahdhib)؛ أقطعت الرجل إقطاعا كأنه طائفة قد قطعت من بلد؛ أقطعت فلانا قضبانا من الكرم إذا أذنت له في قطعها (maqayis)

  المقطع كل شيء يقطع به؛ قاطع فلان وفلان سيفيهما أي نظرا أيهما أقطع؛ القطع نصل صغير يجعل في السهم (ayn)؛ المقطع ما يقطع به الشئ؛ القطع نصل قصير عريض السهم (sihah)؛ سيف قاطع وقطاع ومقطع؛ كل شيء يقطع به فهو مقطع؛ القطاع لا القاطع؛ القطع من النصال القصير العريض (tahdhib)؛ القطع النصل من السهام العريض كأنه لما بري قطع (maqayis)
- **B015** kesilmiş donatı nesnesi — kesik uçlu veya şeritlerden yapılmış kamçı · eyer altına serilen yaygı
  القطيع السوط المقطوع طرفه؛ القطع من الثياب ضرب منها (ayn)؛ القطع طنفسة يجعلها الراكب تحته؛ القطيع السوط (sihah)؛ القطع طنفسة تكون تحت الرحل؛ القطيع السوط المتقطع؛ سمي قطيعا لأنه يقطع أربع طاقات ثم يلوي (tahdhib)؛ القطيع السوط؛ القطع الطنفسة تلقى على الرحل وكأنها سميت بذلك لأن ناسجها يقطعها من غيرها (maqayis)
- **B016** hayvan sürüsü [kalıp] — koyun veya benzeri hayvan sürüsü
  القطيع طائفة من الغنم والنعم ونحوها (ayn)؛ القطيع الطائفة من البقر والغنم؛ اقتطعت قطيعا من غنم فلان (sihah)؛ القطيع من الغنم جمعه قطعان كالصرمة والفرقة (mufradat)؛ القطيع القطعة من الغنم (maqayis)

  الفرس الجواد يقطع الخيل إذا خلفها ومضى؛ الأرنب السريعة مقطعة النياط؛ جاءت الخيل مقطوطعات أي سراعا بعضها في إثر بعض (ayn)؛ قطع الفرس الخيل تقطيعا أي خلفها ومضى؛ جاءت الخيل مقطوطعات أي سراعا بعضها في إثر بعض (sihah)؛ يقال للأرنب السريعة مقطعة النياط ومقطعة الأسحار؛ الفرس الجواد يقطع الخيل تقطيعا؛ ليس فيكم من تقطع عليه الأعناق مثل أبي بكر (tahdhib)؛ مقطعة النياط الأرنب؛ قطع الفرس الخيل تقطيعا خلفها ومضى؛ جاءت الخيل مقطوطعات أي سراعا (maqayis)

  منقطع القرين في الكرم والسخاء إذا لم يكن له مثل؛ منقطع العقال في الشر والخبث (ayn)؛ فلان منقطع القرين في سخاء أو غيره (sihah)؛ فلان منقطع القرين إذا لم يكن له مثل في سخاء أو فضل (tahdhib)؛ فلان منقطع القرين في سخاء أو غيره (maqayis)

  القطيع شبه النظير؛ هذا قطيع هذا أي شبهه في خلقه وقده؛ حسن التقطيع أي القد (ayn)؛ فلان قطيع فلان أي شبيهه في قده وخلقه؛ شيء حسن التقطيع إذا كان حسن القد (tahdhib)

  أقطع ضعف عن النكاح؛ قطيع القيام أي منقطع إذا أراد القيام من ثقل أو سمنة وربما من شدة ضعفه (ayn)؛ فلان قطيع القيام إذا وصف بالضعف أو السمن؛ أقطع الرجل إذا انقطعت حجته وبكتوه بالحق فلم يجب (sihah)؛ رجل قطيع القيام إذا كان ضعيفا؛ أقطع الرجل إذا لم يرد النساء؛ أقطع كلام الرجل إذا بكتوه بالحق فلم يقدر على الجواب (tahdhib)؛ جارية قطيع القيام كأنها من سمنها تنقطع عنه (maqayis)

# s056 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1 Decisive Impact as Material and Social Redistribution
- Semantic invariant: A single unavoidable occurrence breaks prior stability and reallocates both physical and social position.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:1-11 moves from the وقوع of the event through earth-shaking and mountain dispersal to lowering, raising, threefold sorting, precedence, and nearness.
- Surprising reach: The vertical mechanics of impact become a social mechanism: compact height is pulverized while rank is lowered, raised, sided, raced, and brought into royal proximity.

#### Subchannel A. Solid height becomes dispersed matter
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A severe arrival strikes the lower plane, agitates a solid elevation, grinds it, and broadcasts it as fine airborne matter.
- Active motifs: severe descending event `quranic:root_001675:B002/m01`; audible impact `quranic:root_001675:B004/m01`; lower terrestrial plane `quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`; violent shaking `quranic:root_000541:B001/m01`; raised solid mass `quranic:root_000217:B001/m01`; crumbling and mixing `quranic:root_000115:B003/m01`; fine dispersed dust `quranic:root_001576:B002/m01`; scattering and spreading `quranic:root_000083:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:1-2 `وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ/وَقْعَتِ`; 56:4 `رُجَّتِ/رَجًّا`, `أَرْضُ`; 56:5 `جِبَالُ`, `بُسَّتِ/بَسًّا`; 56:6 `هَبَآءً`, `مُّنۢبَثًّا`
- Synthesis: The event is not merely announced; it behaves like an impact process. Ground oscillation removes the mountain's compact, elevated integrity, crumbling and mixing it until the former mass can be spread as airborne particles.

#### Subchannel B. Vertical reversal becomes ranked approach
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Positions are lowered or elevated, sorted into three classes, polarized by side, and resolved through precedence into privileged nearness.
- Active motifs: descent after elevation `quranic:root_000426:B001/m01`; physical raising `quranic:root_000582:B001/m01`; elevation of rank `quranic:root_000582:B002/m01`; threefold number `quranic:root_000203:B001/m01`; class or type `quranic:root_000652:B004/m01`; left side `quranic:root_000772:B001/m01`; ill omen `quranic:root_000772:B003/m01`; blessing `quranic:root_001698:B001/m01`; right side `quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`; precedence in a race `quranic:root_000671:B001/m01`; royal intimates `quranic:root_001212:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:3 `خَافِضَةٌ/رَّافِعَةٌ`; 56:7 `أَزْوَٰجًا`, `ثَلَٰثَةً`; 56:8 `مَيْمَنَةِ`; 56:9 `مَشْـَٔمَةِ`; 56:10 `سَّٰبِقُونَ`; 56:11 `مُقَرَّبُونَ`
- Synthesis: Physical reversal supplies the passage's grammar of status. Three classes are not a static catalog: side assigns auspice, precedence functions like a race, and the foremost finish not merely ahead but inside the circle of a sovereign's intimates.

### 2. P1 Reward as a Protected, Circulating Court
- Semantic invariant: Recompense is materialized as an ordered court whose enclosure, service, intake, beauty, and speech are protected from decay, depletion, and antagonism.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:11-26 presents the near ones, enclosed gardens, arranged seats, circulating servants, food and drink, guarded brilliance, recompense, and peaceful speech.
- Surprising reach: The scene combines royal access, furnishing craft, controlled circulation, non-depleting consumption, and a regulated speech environment rather than offering an undifferentiated luxury inventory.

#### Subchannel A. Cohorts installed in a face-to-face court
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Differently sized temporal cohorts enter a protected garden-court and take ordered, interwoven seats facing one another.
- Active motifs: royal intimates `quranic:root_001212:B004/m01`; tree-screened garden `quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`; good and easeful condition `quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`; gathered cohort `quranic:root_000204:B001/m01`; temporal priority `quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`; small number `quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`; later cohort `quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`; settled couch or throne `quranic:root_000697:B011/m01`; interwoven construction `quranic:root_001658:B001/m01`; reclining support `quranic:root_001678:B004/m01`; mutual facing `quranic:root_001198:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:11 `مُقَرَّبُونَ`; 56:12 `جَنَّٰتِ`, `نَّعِيمِ`; 56:13 `ثُلَّةٌ`, `أَوَّلِينَ`; 56:14 `قَلِيلٌ`, `ءَاخِرِينَ`; 56:15 `سُرُرٍ`, `مَّوْضُونَةٍ`; 56:16 `مُّتَّكِـِٔينَ`, `مُتَقَٰبِلِينَ`
- Synthesis: Nearness opens into a formal social interior. Earlier and later cohorts are counted, then placed on tightly constructed supports in mutual view, turning blessed enclosure into an ordered audience rather than private isolation.

#### Subchannel B. Perpetual service and drink without drain
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Enduring young attendants circulate open drinking vessels from a running source while both bodily impairment and exhaustion of supply are blocked.
- Active motifs: circuit around a center `quranic:root_000957:B001/m01`; young servant `quranic:root_001683:B004/m01`; enduring state `quranic:root_000429:B001/m01`; handleless cup `quranic:root_001328:B001/m01`; cup with its drink `quranic:root_001277:B001/m01`; running spring `quranic:root_001069:B006/m01`; headache or splitting pain `quranic:root_000850:B007/m01`; loss of reason through intoxication `quranic:root_001491:B003/m01`; exhaustion of drink `quranic:root_001491:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:17 `يَطُوفُ`, `وِلْدَٰنٌ`, `مُّخَلَّدُونَ`; 56:18 `أَكْوَابٍ`, `كَأْسٍ`, `مَّعِينٍ`; 56:19 `يُصَدَّعُ`, `يُنزِفُ`
- Synthesis: Circulation is closed as choreography but open as supply. The attendants and vessels keep moving around the seated center, while the spring neither runs out nor exacts the cognitive and bodily cost normally associated with drinking.

#### Subchannel C. Selection joins appetite to provision
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Desired nourishment is selected rather than imposed, joining discernment, fruit, hunted flesh, and appetite in one provisioning act.
- Active motifs: edible fruit `quranic:root_001174:B002/m01`; choosing the preferred good `quranic:root_000452:B003/m01`; edible flesh `quranic:root_001348:B001/m01`; bird in flight `quranic:root_000962:B001/m01`; appetite toward the desired `quranic:root_000825:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:20 `فَٰكِهَةٍ`, `يَتَخَيَّرُ`; 56:21 `لَحْمِ`, `طَيْرٍ`, `يَشْتَهُ`
- Synthesis: The food scene is organized by agency at the point of reception: discernment selects the fruit and appetite selects the flesh. Provision answers a formed desire instead of reducing abundance to sheer quantity.

#### Subchannel D. Brilliance held inside protection
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Contrasting, expansive eyes and pearl-like luminosity are preserved inside a protective casing.
- Active motifs: intense black-white eye contrast `quranic:root_000369:B001/m01`; wide and beautiful eyes `quranic:root_001069:B016/m01`; pearl as precious object `quranic:root_001337:B001/m01`; pearl-like shining `quranic:root_001337:B002/m01`; protective enclosure `quranic:root_001324:B001/m01`; likeness between forms `quranic:root_001397:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:22 `حُورٌ`, `عِينٌ`; 56:23 `أَمْثَٰلِ`, `لُّؤْلُؤِ`, `مَكْنُونِ`
- Synthesis: Beauty is rendered as a high-contrast visual object whose brightness survives because it is enclosed. The pearl comparison therefore conveys not only appearance but guarded value and unhandled preservation.

#### Subchannel E. Compensation closes accusation into peace
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Deliberate work receives matching recompense, and the resulting social soundscape excludes discarded or guilt-loading speech in favor of safety and peace.
- Active motifs: matching recompense `quranic:root_000244:B001/m01`; intentional work `quranic:root_001046:B001/m01`; hearing speech `quranic:root_000741:B001/m01`; discarded speech `quranic:root_001361:B001/m01`; foul or accusatory speech `quranic:root_001361:B002/m01`; loading guilt onto another `quranic:root_000013:B004/m01`; articulated utterance `quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`; safety from harm `quranic:root_000737:B001/m01`; peace opposed to conflict `quranic:root_000737:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:24 `جَزَآءًۢ`, `يَعْمَلُ`; 56:25 `يَسْمَعُ`, `لَغْوًا`, `تَأْثِيمًا`; 56:26 `قِيلًا`, `سَلَٰمًا`
- Synthesis: The court's moral economy matches outcome to purposeful action, then removes the verbal mechanisms that reopen conflict: noise without value, obscenity, and imputed guilt. What remains audible is a repeated declaration of intactness and peace.

### 3. P2 A Maintained Habitat of Uninterrupted Provision
- Semantic invariant: Blessed abundance appears as a deliberately maintained habitat whose plants, water system, furnishings, and access remain serviceable and unobstructed.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:28-35 joins de-thorned and stacked vegetation, extended shade, poured water, uncut fruit, raised bedding, and fresh origination.
- Surprising reach: The lexical network exposes horticultural maintenance, irrigation hardware, and textile preparation beneath the surface garden.

#### Subchannel A. Orchard maintenance produces accessible fruit
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Thorn removal, green pruning, palm tending, and ordered stacking turn living growth into reachable, edible abundance.
- Active motifs: removal of thorns `quranic:root_000417:B002/m01`; cutting green growth `quranic:root_000417:B003/m01`; fruit-bearing tree `quranic:root_000944:B001/m01`; stacked natural bodies `quranic:root_001515:B004/m01`; young palms `quranic:root_000248:B006/m01`; palm hearts and shoots `quranic:root_001286:B006/m01`; pruning palm fronds `quranic:root_000996:B018/m01`; edible fruit `quranic:root_001174:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:28 `مَّخْضُودٍ`; 56:29 `طَلْحٍ`, `مَّنضُودٍ`; 56:32 `فَٰكِهَةٍ`, `كَثِيرَةٍ`; 56:36 `جَعَلْ`; 56:37 `عُرُبًا`
- Synthesis: The orchard is latent with labor. Sharpness is removed, wet growth is selectively cut, palm structures are tended, and fruiting bodies are arranged, so ease of access emerges from cultivation rather than from a shapeless mass of vegetation.

#### Subchannel B. Extended cover and replenished flow block interruption
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Shade extends as protective cover while water is delivered and replenished; cessation and obstruction are named only to be negated.
- Active motifs: shade as cover `quranic:root_000966:B001/m01`; protective shelter `quranic:root_000966:B004/m01`; longitudinal extension `quranic:root_001407:B001/m01`; one water source replenishing another `quranic:root_001407:B003/m01`; water itself `quranic:root_001458:B001/m01`; delivery by watering or pouring `quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`; pouring and surface flow `quranic:root_000723:B001/m01`; cessation of supply `quranic:root_001240:B005/m01`; obstructing barrier `quranic:root_001448:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:30 `ظِلٍّ`, `مَّمْدُودٍ`; 56:31 `مَآءٍ`, `مَّسْكُوبٍ`; 56:33 `مَقْطُوعَةٍ`, `مَمْنُوعَةٍ`
- Synthesis: Canopy and water form one continuity system: cover stretches, water travels, and one body of water can feed another. The negated cutoff and barrier define abundance operationally as provision that remains connected to its users.

#### Subchannel C. Basin and waterskin keep water serviceable
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A basin is founded, a split waterskin is splinted, and water is routed to a designated pouring place.
- Active motifs: basin sides and foundation `quranic:root_001502:B007/m01`; waterskin repair splint `quranic:root_000723:B010/m01`; delivery of water `quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`; designated pouring or irrigation place `quranic:root_000723:B004/m01`; replenishing flow `quranic:root_001407:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:30 `مَّمْدُودٍ`; 56:31 `مَآءٍ`, `مَّسْكُوبٍ`; 56:35 `أَنشَأْ/إِنشَآءً`
- Synthesis: The water scene extends into maintenance technology. Containment begins with the basin's first structural members; portability is preserved by repairing the skin; delivery ends at a prepared locus. The latent mechanism makes uninterrupted water depend on sound interfaces.

#### Subchannel D. A raised interior is spread, stacked, and tailored
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A prepared floor supports bedding and stacked household goods while flowing cloth is cut and fitted into a raised interior.
- Active motifs: spreading and preparation `quranic:root_001143:B001/m01`; bedding and household furnishing `quranic:root_001143:B002/m01`; physical elevation `quranic:root_000582:B001/m01`; stacked household goods `quranic:root_001515:B002/m01`; furnishing rack or support `quranic:root_001515:B003/m01`; fine flowing cloth `quranic:root_000723:B003/m01`; cutting and tailoring cloth `quranic:root_001240:B012/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:29 `مَّنضُودٍ`; 56:31 `مَّسْكُوبٍ`; 56:33 `مَقْطُوعَةٍ`; 56:34 `فُرُشٍ`, `مَّرْفُوعَةٍ`
- Synthesis: Raised bedding belongs to a complete furnishing operation: surfaces are prepared, goods are stacked on supports, and cloth is allowed to flow before being cut to form. The garden's ease therefore acquires an interior architecture.

### 4. P2 Companionship Remade as Firstness and Parity
- Semantic invariant: A new act of formation removes prior wear and establishes companionship through renewed firstness, affection, and equality of age.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:35-40 describes fresh origination, transformed firstness, affectionate peers, and cohorts from both earlier and later peoples.
- Surprising reach: Creation vocabulary does relational work: it remakes not only bodies but the conditions of mutuality.

#### Subchannel A. Origination restores youthful firstness
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A created form is brought into a new state characterized by youthful growth, beginning, and untouched firstness.
- Active motifs: creation and origination `quranic:root_001502:B006/m01`; growth and rearing of youth `quranic:root_001502:B002/m01`; making a thing `quranic:root_000248:B001/m01`; transforming it into a state `quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`; first or newly ripened condition `quranic:root_000143:B002/m01`; untouched firstness `quranic:root_000143:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:35 `أَنشَأْ/إِنشَآءً`; 56:36 `جَعَلْ`, `أَبْكَارًا`
- Synthesis: The operation proceeds from origination to transformation. Youth is not merely retained; a new state is deliberately produced, and firstness becomes the condition that marks the result.

#### Subchannel B. Affection joins equals across temporal cohorts
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Loving companionship is organized among equal-age peers and extends to blessed cohorts drawn from both temporal ends.
- Active motifs: affectionate spouse `quranic:root_000996:B005/m01`; equal-age companion `quranic:root_000178:B004/m01`; companionship and abiding association `quranic:root_000844:B001/m01`; blessing `quranic:root_001698:B001/m01`; gathered cohort `quranic:root_000204:B001/m01`; temporal priority `quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`; later cohort `quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:27, 56:38 `أَصْحَٰبُ/أَصْحَٰبِ`, `يَمِينِ`; 56:37 `عُرُبًا`, `أَتْرَابًا`; 56:39 `ثُلَّةٌ`, `أَوَّلِينَ`; 56:40 `ثُلَّةٌ`, `ءَاخِرِينَ`
- Synthesis: The social unit is neither hierarchy nor ancestry but reciprocal affection among peers. Equal age supplies symmetry, while the two large cohorts show that this relational parity can gather people from opposite ends of historical sequence.

### 5. P3 Heat That Penetrates Shelter and Intake
- Semantic invariant: Heat defeats every ordinary boundary: it enters through the air, occupies the would-be shelter, fills the interior body, and converts drinking into further thirst.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:41-44 establishes the thermal enclosure, and 56:51-56 carries it through eating, belly-filling, hot drinking, and hostile hospitality.
- Surprising reach: Pores, jaw-space, throat channels, vessel capacity, absorbent sand, and diseased camels turn punishment into a continuous transport mechanism from atmosphere to interior.

#### Subchannel A. A canopy that conducts heat instead of refuge
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Encompassing hot wind enters through narrow openings beneath a black overhead cover that withholds both cooling and beneficent shelter.
- Active motifs: enclosure from all sides `quranic:root_000819:B001/m01`; penetrating hot wind `quranic:root_000743:B004/m01`; narrow bodily openings `quranic:root_000743:B001/m01`; black smoke and soot `quranic:root_000001:B001/m01`; overhead canopy `quranic:root_000966:B003/m01`; cooling and coldness `quranic:root_000103:B001/m01`; beneficent honor `quranic:root_001294:B001/m01`; sweat and heat exposure `quranic:root_000001:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:41 `شِّمَالِ`; 56:42 `سَمُومٍ`, `حَمِيمٍ`; 56:43 `ظِلٍّ`, `يَحْمُومٍ`; 56:44 `بَارِدٍ`, `كَرِيمٍ`
- Synthesis: The scene reverses shelter's function. A cover still encloses, but its material is blackened heat; the wind is defined by its ability to pass through fine openings, and the missing coolness and generosity make the canopy an exposure chamber.

#### Subchannel B. Solid intake is driven from jaw to overfull belly
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Tree matter is taken into an opened jaw, forced toward the throat, and packed into an interior container beyond comfortable fullness.
- Active motifs: eating `quranic:root_000043:B001/m01`; tree with a trunk `quranic:root_000777:B001/m01`; gap between the jaws `quranic:root_000777:B005/m01`; swallowing to the throat `quranic:root_000636:B002/m01`; filling to vessel capacity `quranic:root_001441:B001/m01`; inner belly or cavity `quranic:root_000128:B001/m01`; overfull or afflicted belly `quranic:root_000128:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:52 `ءَاكِلُونَ`, `شَجَرٍ`, `زَقُّومٍ`; 56:53 `مَالِـُٔونَ`, `بُطُونَ`
- Synthesis: The ingestion sequence is mechanically explicit. A branching source becomes mouthful, the jaw becomes an aperture, swallowing drives the material inward, and the belly behaves like a vessel filled to a harmful limit.

#### Subchannel C. Hot drinking renews rather than resolves thirst
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Hot liquid passes through the throat in repeated draughts, while camel disease and absorbent sand model intake that cannot produce satiety.
- Active motifs: drinking liquid `quranic:root_000783:B001/m01`; throat channels `quranic:root_000783:B006/m01`; heated water `quranic:root_000001:B002/m01`; unquenchable thirst `quranic:root_001614:B001/m01`; dry sand that swallows water `quranic:root_001614:B002/m01`; camel thirst-disease `quranic:root_001614:B003/m01`; hostile guest provision `quranic:root_001492:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:54 `شَٰرِبُونَ`, `حَمِيمِ`; 56:55 `شَٰرِبُونَ/شُرْبَ`, `هِيمِ`; 56:56 `نُزُلُ`
- Synthesis: Drinking ceases to be restorative. The throat transmits heated liquid to a body modeled simultaneously as diseased livestock and water-devouring sand, so every draught reproduces the deficit; naming it guest provision completes the inversion of hospitality.

### 6. P3 Entrenched Denial Meets Scheduled Assembly
- Semantic invariant: A self-reinforcing stance of indulgence and denial is answered by the equally firm but external order of reanimation, collection, and appointed reckoning.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:45-51 moves from luxury and persistence through denial of resurrection to the collection of first and last at a known appointment.
- Surprising reach: The contrast is not simply belief versus disbelief; it is one fixation of will displaced by a stronger fixation of time, place, and bodily reassembly.

#### Subchannel A. Indulgence hardens into oath and assertion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Unrestricted luxury produces fixed resolve, breached obligation, and repeated false speech.
- Active motifs: luxury that releases its possessor from restraint `quranic:root_000179:B001/m01`; persistent resolve `quranic:root_000856:B002/m01`; grave sin and deviation `quranic:root_000359:B001/m01`; breach of an oath `quranic:root_000359:B002/m01`; articulated claim `quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`; falsehood opposed to truth `quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:45 `مُتْرَفِينَ`; 56:46 `يُصِرُّ`, `حِنثِ`; 56:47 `يَقُولُ`; 56:51 `مُكَذِّبُونَ`
- Synthesis: Ease is shown as a social condition that removes constraint, after which persistence becomes an operation: resolve is tied, obligation is broken, and the resulting assertion is held against contradiction.

#### Subchannel B. Dormant bodily material is roused
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Lost life, soil, and hard bone are treated as a recumbent state from which prior generations can be stirred.
- Active motifs: loss of life and force `quranic:root_001454:B001/m01`; soil and dust `quranic:root_000178:B001/m01`; hard bone `quranic:root_001029:B005/m01`; rousing a sleeper or recumbent animal `quranic:root_000129:B001/m01`; fathers and progenitors `quranic:root_000007:B001/m01`; temporal priority `quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:47 `مِتْ`, `تُرَابًا`, `عِظَٰمًا`, `مَبْعُوثُونَ`; 56:48 `ءَابَآؤُ`, `أَوَّلُونَ`
- Synthesis: The denied transformation is lexically less like rebuilding from nothing than awakening something laid down. Dust and bone are the dormant material, while بعث supplies the kinetic reversal from recumbency to movement.

#### Subchannel C. All temporal ends converge at a fixed appointment
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Earlier and later populations are collected into one assembly whose time, severe day, and accounting function are fixed and known.
- Active motifs: temporal priority `quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`; later population `quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`; collecting dispersed persons `quranic:root_000259:B001/m01`; fixing a temporal limit `quranic:root_001671:B002/m01`; appointed meeting time `quranic:root_001671:B003/m01`; severe event-day `quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`; known fact `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; reckoning and requital `quranic:root_000504:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:49 `أَوَّلِينَ`, `ءَاخِرِينَ`; 56:50 `مَجْمُوعُونَ`, `مِيقَٰتِ`, `يَوْمٍ`, `مَّعْلُومٍ`; 56:56 `يَوْمَ`, `دِّينِ`
- Synthesis: Sequence collapses into simultaneity: first and last lose their temporal separation when collection places them under one known limit. The appointment is therefore both calendar and court, a time that gathers in order to account.

### 7. P4 Life as Measured Generation and Replaceable Form
- Semantic invariant: Embodied life is a measured production whose material, duration, form, and replacement remain under an agency beyond its human transmitters.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:57-62 interrogates creation from emitted fluid, apportions death, and asserts replacement and another origination from the known first one.
- Surprising reach: Measuring leather before cutting, reproductive seed, visible pregnancy, and substitution of forms make creation legible as calibrated manufacture without reducing it to mechanism alone.

#### Subchannel A. Reproductive material is calibrated into form and duration
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Emitted reproductive material enters a process of prior measurement, creation, formal completion, allotted lifespan, and inescapable end.
- Active motifs: seminal material from which form is measured `quranic:root_001450:B002/m01`; measuring before cutting or acting `quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`; origination of a created thing `quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`; completed and balanced form `quranic:root_000434:B003/m01`; amount and terminal limit `quranic:root_001205:B001/m01`; planned calibration `quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`; interval distributed among parties `quranic:root_000170:B002/m01`; loss of life `quranic:root_001454:B001/m01`; failure to outrun a pursuer `quranic:root_000671:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:57 `خَلَقْ`; 56:58 `تُمْنُ`; 56:59 `تَخْلُقُ/خَٰلِقُونَ`; 56:60 `قَدَّرْ`, `بَيْنَ`, `مَوْتَ`, `مَسْبُوقِينَ`
- Synthesis: Generation is portrayed as more than emission. The material is measured before form, completed into a balanced body, and assigned a bounded interval; death is the limit that cannot be outrun, making lifespan part of the original calibration.

#### Subchannel B. Replacement preserves relation while changing form
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: One bearer can stand in another's place, an image can change while relation remains, and a new origination can follow the first.
- Active motifs: one thing standing in another's place `quranic:root_000095:B001/m01`; change of outward form `quranic:root_000095:B002/m01`; likeness or counterpart `quranic:root_001397:B001/m01`; fresh origination `quranic:root_001502:B006/m01`; disclosed knowledge `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; first in sequence `quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`; recollection after forgetting `quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:61 `نُّبَدِّلَ`, `أَمْثَٰلَ`, `نُنشِئَ`, `تَعْلَمُ`; 56:62 `عَلِمْ`, `نَّشْأَةَ`, `أُولَىٰ`, `تَذَكَّرُ`
- Synthesis: Substitution does not require simple duplication. A counterpart preserves intelligible relation while alteration changes the presented form; knowledge of the first origination supplies the model by which another can be remembered as possible.

#### Subchannel C. Human seed and agricultural seed share a generative frame
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Semen functions as seed, descends as reproductive fluid, begins pregnancy, and becomes visible in the body.
- Active motifs: male seed as crop seed `quranic:root_000630:B004/m01`; seminal material `quranic:root_001450:B002/m01`; emitted male fluid `quranic:root_001492:B009/m01`; onset of pregnancy `quranic:root_001502:B009/m01`; visible signs of pregnancy `quranic:root_000531:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:58 `رَءَيْ`, `تُمْنُ`; 56:61-62 `نُنشِئَ/نَّشْأَةَ`; 56:64 `تَزْرَعُ/زَّٰرِعُونَ`; 56:69 `أَنزَلْ/مُنزِلُونَ`
- Synthesis: The lexical field explicitly joins the reproductive and agricultural demonstrations. Seed is cast in both womb and soil, pregnancy is the hidden onset of growth, and visible enlargement is the point at which an unseen generative process declares itself.

### 8. P4 Provision Chains Humans Use but Do Not Originate
- Semantic invariant: Food, water, and fire arrive through multi-stage transformations that humans initiate or use locally but cannot secure at the level of growth, descent, or latent energy.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:63-73 repeats the contrast between human handling and divine origination across crop, drinking water, and fire.
- Surprising reach: Debt, famine, cloud husbandry, minimal-input flourishing, tinder, and traveler supplies expose the operational dependencies inside ordinary provision.

#### Subchannel A. Field labor can terminate in harvest or ruin
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Seed is cast into worked ground and grown, but the crop can be broken into dry fragments, converting expected gain into persistent loss and deprivation.
- Active motifs: casting seed and preparing soil `quranic:root_000303:B002/m01`; trampled and worked ground `quranic:root_000303:B008/m01`; growing a crop `quranic:root_000630:B001/m01`; breaking dry matter into fragments `quranic:root_000337:B001/m01`; famine that breaks people and property `quranic:root_000337:B003/m01`; financial liability `quranic:root_001081:B001/m01`; abiding ruin `quranic:root_001081:B003/m01`; deprivation of an expected good `quranic:root_000313:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:63 `تَحْرُثُ`; 56:64 `تَزْرَعُ/زَّٰرِعُونَ`; 56:65 `حُطَٰمًا`; 56:66 `مُغْرَمُونَ`; 56:67 `مَحْرُومُونَ`
- Synthesis: The field scene separates labor from outcome. Humans can disturb earth and cast seed, but growth can be reversed into brittle debris; at that point agricultural failure becomes an economic relation of liability and an existential state of being shut out.

#### Subchannel B. Cloud delivery can become drink or brine
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Water is gathered in cloud, sent downward for drinking, and remains subject to conversion into bitter salinity; gratitude answers the contingency of the chain.
- Active motifs: water itself `quranic:root_001458:B001/m01`; rain cloud `quranic:root_001419:B001/m01`; causing a thing to descend and arrive `quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`; intensely saline and bitter water `quranic:root_000014:B003/m01`; recognition of a benefaction `quranic:root_000810:B001/m01`; flourishing from little input `quranic:root_000810:B002/m01`; intensified rain `quranic:root_000810:B005/m01`; cloud that nurtures vegetation `quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:68 `مَآءَ`, `تَشْرَبُ`; 56:69 `مُزْنِ`, `أَنزَلْ/مُنزِلُونَ`; 56:70 `أُجَاجًا`, `تَشْكُرُ`; 56:74 `رَبِّ`
- Synthesis: The drink is the endpoint of a meteorological delivery system, not an isolated object. Cloud holds and descends it, salinity remains a possible transformation, and gratitude is the human operation suited to a chain whose decisive transitions are not human-controlled.

#### Subchannel C. Latent fire becomes a travel provision
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Fire hidden in wood is struck out, raised, and converted into a remembered utility for people in empty country.
- Active motifs: burning fire `quranic:root_001564:B002/m01`; latent fire issuing from a firestick `quranic:root_001642:B002/m01`; woody tree `quranic:root_000777:B001/m01`; origination `quranic:root_001502:B006/m01`; reminder that restores presence `quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`; useful supply for needs `quranic:root_001395:B003/m01`; empty land with scarce provision `quranic:root_001274:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:71 `نَّارَ`, `تُورُ`; 56:72 `شَجَرَتَ`, `أَنشَأْ/مُنشِـُٔونَ`; 56:73 `تَذْكِرَةً`, `مَتَٰعًا`, `مُقْوِينَ`
- Synthesis: The tree is both material and energy store. Striking releases a hidden flame, and that flame becomes portable benefit precisely where other supplies are scarce; its usefulness also makes it a mnemonic object.

### 9. P4 Demonstration Becomes Remembrance and Naming
- Semantic invariant: Repeated attention to familiar processes is converted into reflective knowledge, recollection, and a final act of naming and magnifying their sustaining Lord.
- Surface relation: direct; the repeated `أَفَرَءَيْتُم` of 56:58, 63, 68, and 71 culminates in remembrance at 56:73 and glorification at 56:74.
- Surprising reach: Sight operates as interrogation, deliberation, and disclosure; naming is not a label added afterward but the verbal completion of demonstrated dependence.

#### Subchannel A. Seeing is an interrogative operation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Visual attention is repeatedly redirected into deliberation, disclosure, knowledge, and recollection.
- Active motifs: sensory seeing `quranic:root_000531:B001/m01`; reflective judgment `quranic:root_000531:B002/m01`; interrogative “tell me” `quranic:root_000531:B013/m01`; disclosed knowledge `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; recollection after forgetting `quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`; reminder that restores presence `quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:58, 56:63, 56:68, 56:71 `رَءَيْ`; 56:61-62 `تَعْلَمُ/عَلِمْ`; 56:62 `تَذَكَّرُ`; 56:73 `تَذْكِرَةً`
- Synthesis: The refrain does not request passive looking. Each familiar object is made to answer a question about agency, so perception becomes reasoning; repeated demonstrations accumulate into remembered knowledge.

#### Subchannel B. Recognition resolves into an elevated name
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The sustaining owner and cultivator is named, elevated, and declared beyond defect.
- Active motifs: transcendence and exoneration `quranic:root_000666:B002/m01`; name as public designation `quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`; elevation `quranic:root_000745:B001/m01`; lordship and ownership `quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`; nurture, repair, and completion `quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`; magnitude and power `quranic:root_001029:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:74 `سَبِّحْ`, `ٱسْمِ`, `رَبِّ`, `عَظِيمِ`
- Synthesis: The sequence of demonstrations ends in a compact verbal act. The name identifies the owner of the processes, lordship gathers their nurture and completion, greatness raises their scale, and glorification removes any attribution of defect.

### 10. P5 Ordered Placement and Guarded Transmission
- Semantic invariant: Authority is expressed through exact placement, gathered inscription, protective enclosure, and controlled contact during transmission.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:75-80 moves from star positions and oath to a noble recitation in a concealed book, restricted touch, and descent from the Lord.
- Surprising reach: Cosmic location and codex manufacture share an invariant of components put in place; stitching, casing, washing, and delivery materialize textual preservation.

#### Subchannel A. Celestial positions establish an attestive order
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Rising stars occupy fixed falling or landing positions, and an oath marks the magnitude of an order available to knowledge.
- Active motifs: oath `quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`; falling and fixation in place `quranic:root_001675:B001/m01`; stars and celestial stations `quranic:root_001475:B001/m01`; rising into visibility `quranic:root_001475:B002/m01`; prominent marker or path `quranic:root_001475:B006/m01`; disclosed knowledge `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; magnitude and power `quranic:root_001029:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:75 `أُقْسِمُ`, `مَوَٰقِعِ`, `نُّجُومِ`; 56:76 `قَسَمٌ`, `تَعْلَمُ`, `عَظِيمٌ`
- Synthesis: The oath points to a patterned sky in which appearance and placement coincide. Stars rise as visible markers yet are known through their stations, so celestial order becomes the attestive frame for the textual order that follows.

#### Subchannel B. Recitation is gathered into a stitched enclosure
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Speech is gathered and recited, joined into writing, and held in a protective book casing.
- Active motifs: gathering components `quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`; reading and recitation `quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`; noble excellence `quranic:root_001294:B001/m01`; joining and stitching leather `quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`; ordered writing `quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`; protective casing `quranic:root_001324:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:77 `قُرْءَانٌ`, `كَرِيمٌ`; 56:78 `كِتَٰبٍ`, `مَّكْنُونٍ`
- Synthesis: Reading gathers utterance; writing joins signs; bookmaking joins material edges; enclosure preserves the assembled result. The latent stitched-codex scene gives physical form to the movement from recited plurality to guarded unity.

#### Subchannel C. Cleansed contact governs descent and access
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Bodily contact is conditioned by cleansing, while the text itself arrives through an ordered act of delivery from its sovereign source.
- Active motifs: tactile contact `quranic:root_001423:B001/m01`; purity from defilement `quranic:root_000953:B001/m01`; washing with water `quranic:root_000953:B003/m01`; sending down and delivering `quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`; lordship and ownership `quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:79 `يَمَسُّ`, `مُطَهَّرُونَ`; 56:80 `تَنزِيلٌ`, `رَّبِّ`
- Synthesis: Access is neither casual handling nor self-authorized possession. Cleansing regulates the receiver's contact, while descent regulates the text's direction of arrival; both sides of transmission are ordered by the source's ownership.

### 11. P5 Authoritative Register and Final Settlement
- Semantic invariant: Allotted provision, recorded obligation, judgment, and verified truth form one administrative scene in which claims are entered and finally settled.
- Surface relation: indirect; 56:75-82 supplies oath, book, provision, and denial, while 56:86-87 and 56:95 name judgment, truthfulness, fixed truth, and certainty.
- Surprising reach: The book can function simultaneously as inscription and register, while provision becomes an allotted share and judgment becomes the settlement of debt and right.

#### Subchannel A. Shares and obligations enter the register
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Portions are divided, names and decrees are entered into a record, allotted provision is issued, and debt comes due for reckoning.
- Active motifs: division into shares `quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`; binding decree `quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`; entry of a name in a register `quranic:root_001283:B004/m01`; allotted provision `quranic:root_000560:B001/m01`; edible maintenance `quranic:root_000560:B002/m01`; financial debt `quranic:root_000504:B003/m01`; reckoning and requital `quranic:root_000504:B002/m01`; a claimant's specific right `quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:75-76 `أُقْسِمُ/قَسَمٌ`; 56:78 `كِتَٰبٍ`; 56:82 `رِزْقَ`; 56:86 `مَدِينِينَ`; 56:95 `حَقُّ`
- Synthesis: The same network that divides an oath's burden can allocate a share, enter a person in a register, and establish an obligation. Provision and judgment are thus not unrelated themes: one issues what is apportioned, the other settles what remains due.

#### Subchannel B. Contested assertion becomes demonstrated certainty
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: False and true claims are opposed, tested by action, established as fixed reality, and brought beyond doubt.
- Active motifs: truth-matching speech `quranic:root_000852:B001/m01`; fulfillment that proves a claim `quranic:root_000852:B004/m01`; falsehood `quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`; reality fixed against falsehood `quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`; binding necessity `quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`; establishment and disclosure of truth `quranic:root_000347:B005/m01`; knowledge with doubt removed `quranic:root_001696:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:82 `تُكَذِّبُ`; 56:87 `صَٰدِقِينَ`; 56:92 `مُكَذِّبِينَ`; 56:95 `حَقُّ`, `يَقِينِ`
- Synthesis: Truth moves through stages: assertion, practical test, establishment, and certainty. The death challenge supplies the test, while the closing formula states the result as both objectively fixed and subjectively beyond doubt.

### 12. P5 The Death Threshold Tests Claimed Autonomy
- Semantic invariant: At the body's terminal boundary, visible observation fails to reach the nearest agency, and inability to reverse the passage exposes subjection to judgment.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:83-87 stages the soul at the throat, watching without sight, hidden proximity, claimed freedom from judgment, and the failed command to return it.
- Surprising reach: The scene distinguishes looking from insight and spatial closeness from perceptibility, then converts a metaphysical claim into a practical reversal test.

#### Subchannel A. Looking cannot cross the terminal boundary
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Life reaches the throat at its appointed time while observers look and wait, yet fail to perceive what is closer than they are.
- Active motifs: arrival at a terminal limit `quranic:root_000151:B001/m01`; throat and breath passage `quranic:root_000350:B001/m01`; appointed time `quranic:root_000382:B001/m01`; moment of death `quranic:root_000382:B002/m01`; directed looking `quranic:root_001520:B001/m01`; waiting for the moment `quranic:root_001520:B002/m01`; spatial or relational nearness `quranic:root_001212:B001/m01`; physical sight `quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`; inward insight `quranic:root_000121:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:83 `بَلَغَتِ`, `حُلْقُومَ`; 56:84 `حِينَئِذٍ`, `تَنظُرُ`; 56:85 `أَقْرَبُ`, `تُبْصِرُ`
- Synthesis: The body's upper passage becomes a boundary of knowledge. Human eyes remain open and directed, but proximity does not guarantee perception; the closest presence lies beyond the observers' visual reach.

#### Subchannel B. Failed return demonstrates subjection
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A claim to stand outside judgment is tested by ordering the departing life to return; inability to restore it falsifies autonomy.
- Active motifs: being other than or outside a class `quranic:root_001119:B005/m01`; subjugation and compelled rule `quranic:root_000504:B004/m01`; return to a prior state `quranic:root_000544:B001/m01`; truth-matching assertion `quranic:root_000852:B001/m01`; fulfillment that proves a claim `quranic:root_000852:B004/m01`; occurrence in a state `quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:86 `كُن`, `غَيْرَ`, `مَدِينِينَ`; 56:87 `تَرْجِعُ`, `كُن`, `صَٰدِقِينَ`
- Synthesis: Freedom from judgment is not debated abstractly. It is operationalized as control over reversal: if the observers are truly unruled, they should restore what has reached the terminal boundary. Their failure discloses the stronger relation of subjection.

### 13. P5 Terminal Classification as Reception
- Semantic invariant: Death resolves identity into one of three receptions, each expressed through a distinct hospitality, social relation, and bodily environment.
- Surface relation: direct; 56:88-96 assigns rest and garden to the near, peace to the right-hand companions, and hot provision and flame to deniers before sealing the division as certain truth.
- Surprising reach: Breeze, scent, plant, greeting, guest provision, and thermal exposure make classification experiential; the closing glorification treats that reception order as a disclosed reality.

#### Subchannel A. The near are received by breath, rest, and growth
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Royal intimates enter a reception of breeze, fragrance, restored breath, vegetation, enclosure, and ease.
- Active motifs: royal intimates `quranic:root_001212:B004/m01`; breeze and moving air `quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`; fragrance and scent `quranic:root_000609:B004/m01`; rest after exhaustion `quranic:root_000609:B007/m01`; fragrant vegetation `quranic:root_000609:B012/m01`; tree-screened garden `quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`; good and easeful state `quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:88 `مُقَرَّبِينَ`; 56:89 `رَوْحٌ/رَيْحَانٌ`, `جَنَّتُ`, `نَعِيمٍ`
- Synthesis: Nearness is answered by a breathable habitat. Air, scent, rest, and plant growth form one restorative ecology inside the garden enclosure, reversing the constricted throat and failed human breath of the preceding scene.

#### Subchannel B. Right-hand companionship arrives as peace
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Blessed side, durable association, and freedom from harm converge in a greeting exchanged among companions.
- Active motifs: companionship and association `quranic:root_000844:B001/m01`; blessing `quranic:root_001698:B001/m01`; right side `quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`; safety from harm `quranic:root_000737:B001/m01`; peace opposed to conflict `quranic:root_000737:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:90-91 `أَصْحَٰبِ`, `يَمِينِ`; 56:91 `سَلَٰمٌ`
- Synthesis: The second reception is social rather than architectural. Side identifies the blessed company, companionship supplies mutual belonging, and the greeting makes safety audible as the relation holding that company together.

#### Subchannel C. Deniers receive predatory hospitality
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Lost and denying guests are met with heated drink and direct exposure to a swelling blaze.
- Active motifs: falsehood `quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`; deviation from the path `quranic:root_000913:B001/m01`; becoming lost or unfindable `quranic:root_000913:B003/m01`; prepared guest provision `quranic:root_001492:B005/m01`; heated water `quranic:root_000001:B002/m01`; entering and suffering fire `quranic:root_000880:B003/m01`; intensely blazing heat `quranic:root_000225:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:92 `مُكَذِّبِينَ`, `ضَّآلِّينَ`; 56:93 `نُزُلٌ`, `حَمِيمٍ`; 56:94 `تَصْلِيَةُ`, `جَحِيمٍ`
- Synthesis: Hospitality remains structurally present but its provisions turn predatory. The lost arrive at a prepared reception whose drink burns and whose lodging is exposure to flame, completing the inversion already named as hostile guest provision in P3.

#### Subchannel D. Certain reception order elicits glorification
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: The threefold outcome is declared fixed and doubtless, then answered by naming and exonerating its sovereign source.
- Active motifs: reality fixed against falsehood `quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`; knowledge with doubt removed `quranic:root_001696:B001/m01`; transcendence and exoneration `quranic:root_000666:B002/m01`; name as public designation `quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`; lordship and ownership `quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`; magnitude and power `quranic:root_001029:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 56:95 `حَقُّ`, `يَقِينِ`; 56:96 `سَبِّحْ`, `ٱسْمِ`, `رَبِّ`, `عَظِيمِ`
- Synthesis: Classification closes as epistemic and liturgical certainty. Its reality is fixed, doubt is removed, and the appropriate response is to name the Lord, magnify the scale of the order, and clear its source of defect.

### 14. P1-P5 Directed Transmission from Disclosure to Arrival
- Semantic invariant: What is sent becomes effective through a directed chain from source and disclosure through carrier, channel, marked route, and intended reception.
- Surface relation: indirect; P1 regulates what is heard and said at 56:25-26, P2 contributes the elevation lexeme at 56:34, P3 names dispatch at resurrection and a known appointment at 56:47-50, P4 repeatedly stages seeing and recognition at 56:58-71, and P5 presents sent-down discourse beside the death threshold at 56:75-83.
- Surprising reach: Oral publication and physical courier movement share one delivery architecture; the wish that unwelcome news be heard without reaching its subject isolates failed arrival as the reversal of successful transmission.

#### Subchannel A. A report is released, heard, and blocked from its subject
- Reading type: mixed
